Existing test-time adaptation (TTA) methods rely on gradient-based updates that require white-box access and need substantial overhead, while training-free alternatives are either static or depend on external guidance. In this paper, we propose Training-Free Test-Time Contrastive Learning TF-TTCL, a training-free adaptation framework that enables a frozen LLM to improve online by distilling supervision from its own inference experiences. Specifically, TF-TTCL implements a dynamic "Explore-Reflect-Steer" loop through three core modules: 1) Semantic Query Augmentation first diversifies problem views via multi-agent role-playing to generate different reasoning trajectories; 2) Contrastive Experience Distillation then captures the semantic gap between superior and inferior trajectories, distilling them into explicit textual rules; and 3) Contextual Rule Retrieval finally activates these stored rules during inference to dynamically steer the frozen LLM toward robust reasoning patterns while avoiding observed errors. Extensive experiments on closed-ended reasoning tasks and open-ended evaluation tasks demonstrate that TF-TTCL consistently outperforms strong zero-shot baselines and representative TTA methods under online evaluation. Code is available at https://github.com/KevinSCUTer/TF-TTCL.

</details>


### [115] [YOCO++: Enhancing YOCO with KV Residual Connections for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13556)
*You Wu,Ziheng Chen,Yizhen Zhang,Haoyi Wu,Chengting Yu,Yuchi Xu,Wenbo Su,Bo Zheng,Kewei Tu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: YOCO++ 是一种改进的跨层KV压缩方法，通过引入加权残差连接，在保持高效推理的同时提升了大语言模型的性能，在50% KV缓存压缩率下达到当前最优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨层KV压缩方法虽能减少内存占用，但通常带来不可忽视的性能下降，因此需要提升此类方法在压缩后的模型表现。

Method: 在YOCO的基础上，提出YOCO++，引入底部各层与底层KV之间的加权残差连接，以增强信息传递并提升模型容量。

Result: 在50% KV缓存压缩率下，YOCO++在跨层KV压缩方法中实现了最先进的性能，并优于标准Transformer模型。

Conclusion: YOCO++有效提升了跨层KV压缩方法的性能，在不增加训练和推理开销的前提下增强了模型表现，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Cross-layer key-value (KV) compression has been found to be effective in efficient inference of large language models (LLMs). Although they reduce the memory consumption of the KV cache, such methods usually introduce non-negligible performance degradation. In this work, we aim to enhance the performance of YOCO, a cross-layer KV compression method that shares the KVs of the middle layer with the top-half layers. We propose YOCO++, an enhanced YOCO that incorporates a weighted residual connection between the KVs of each bottom-half layer and the bottom layer. Compared to YOCO, YOCO++ increases model capacity while maintaining the same training and inference efficiency. Our experiments show that YOCO++ achieves state-of-the-art performance among the cross-layer KV compression methods at a 50% KV cache compression rate, outperforming the standard Transformer.

</details>


### [116] [Foresight Optimization for Strategic Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13592)
*Jiashuo Wang,Jiawen Duan,Jian Wang,Kaitao Song,Chunpu Xu,Johnny K. W. Ho,Fenggang Yu,Wenjie Li,Johan F. Hoorn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Foresight Policy Optimization (FoPO) 的新方法，旨在增强大语言模型在多智能体环境中的战略推理能力，通过整合对手建模原则来显式优化策略，从而提升决策效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于推理的LLM在多智能体环境中缺乏明确的预见性建模，导致其在战略决策方面表现不佳，因此需要一种能够捕捉这种前瞻性特征的方法。

Method: 引入Foresight Policy Optimization (FoPO)，将对手建模融入策略优化中，并构建了两个数据集（Cooperative RSA 和 Competitive Taboo）以在自对弈框架下系统研究FoPO的效果。

Result: 实验表明，FoPO显著提升了不同规模和来源的LLM的战略推理能力，并且训练后的模型在域外战略场景中表现出强大的泛化能力，远超标准的LLM推理优化基线。

Conclusion: FoPO能有效增强LLM在多智能体环境中的战略推理与决策能力，具有良好的泛化性和应用前景。

Abstract: Reasoning capabilities in large language models (LLMs) have generally advanced significantly. However, it is still challenging for existing reasoning-based LLMs to perform effective decision-making abilities in multi-agent environments, due to the absence of explicit foresight modeling. To this end, strategic reasoning, the most fundamental capability to anticipate the counterpart's behaviors and foresee its possible future actions, has been introduced to alleviate the above issues. Strategic reasoning is fundamental to effective decision-making in multi-agent environments, yet existing reasoning enhancement methods for LLMs do not explicitly capture its foresight nature. In this work, we introduce Foresight Policy Optimization (FoPO) to enhance strategic reasoning in LLMs, which integrates opponent modeling principles into policy optimization, thereby enabling explicit consideration of both self-interest and counterpart influence. Specifically, we construct two curated datasets, namely Cooperative RSA and Competitive Taboo, equipped with well-designed rules and moderate difficulty to facilitate a systematic investigation of FoPO in a self-play framework. Our experiments demonstrate that FoPO significantly enhances strategic reasoning across LLMs of varying sizes and origins. Moreover, models trained with FoPO exhibit strong generalization to out-of-domain strategic scenarios, substantially outperforming standard LLM reasoning optimization baselines.

</details>


### [117] [C2: Scalable Rubric-Augmented Reward Modeling from Binary Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13618)
*Akira Kawabata,Saku Sugawara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为C2（Cooperative yet Critical reward modeling）的框架，通过让奖励模型与仅基于二元偏好的评分标准生成器进行批判性协作，显著提升了判断准确性。该方法利用对比学习生成有帮助和误导性的评分标准对，训练出合作性生成器和批判性验证器，在无需外部标注的情况下实现了更可靠、可扩展的奖励建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分标准的验证方法依赖昂贵的人工标注，且低质量评分标准会误导奖励模型；同时缺乏促进生成器与验证器之间有效协作的机制，导致性能受限。

Method: 提出C2框架：首先通过分析评分标准对奖励模型偏好方向的影响，自动生成有帮助和误导性的评分标准对；然后使用这些对比样本来联合训练一个合作性评分标准生成器和一个批判性验证器；在推理时，验证器只采纳其认为有益的评分标准进行判断。

Result: C2在RM-Bench上比基于相同二元偏好的推理奖励模型最高提升6.5分，在AlpacaEval 2.0的长度控制胜率上提升达6.0分；无需外部评分标准标注时，8B规模的奖励模型能达到使用4倍大模型生成评分标准的性能水平。

Conclusion: 通过在评分标准增强的验证中引入有意识的合作机制，C2实现了更可信、可扩展的奖励建模，证明了批判性协作能有效提升奖励模型的可靠性。

Abstract: Rubric-augmented verification guides reward models with explicit evaluation criteria, yielding more reliable judgments than single-model verification. However, most existing methods require costly rubric annotations, limiting scalability. Moreover, we find that rubric generation is vulnerable to a failure of cooperation; low-quality rubrics actively mislead reward models rather than help. Inspired by the principle of cooperative communication, we propose Cooperative yet Critical reward modeling (C2), a framework that significantly improves reward model judgments by having the reward model critically collaborate with a rubric generator trained solely from binary preferences. In C2, we synthesize helpful and misleading rubric pairs by measuring how each rubric shifts the reward model toward or away from the correct preference. Using these contrastive pairs, we train a cooperative rubric generator to propose helpful rubrics, and a critical verifier to assess rubric validity before making its judgment, following only rubrics it deems helpful at inference time. C2 outperforms reasoning reward models trained on the same binary preferences, with gains of up to 6.5 points on RM-Bench and 6.0 points length-controlled win rate on AlpacaEval 2.0. Without external rubric annotations, C2 enables an 8B reward model to match performance achieved with rubrics from a 4$\times$ larger model. Overall, our work demonstrates that eliciting deliberate cooperation in rubric-augmented verification makes reward models more trustworthy in a scalable way.

</details>


### [118] [Syn-TurnTurk: A Synthetic Dataset for Turn-Taking Prediction in Turkish Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13620)
*Ahmet Tuğrul Bayrak,Mustafa Sertaç Türkel,Fatma Nur Korkmaz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于土耳其语对话回合预测的合成数据集Syn-TurnTurk，利用Qwen大语言模型生成包含真实语音特征的数据，并通过多种模型验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现有语音聊天机器人依赖简单的静音检测，常因人类说话中的不规则停顿而打断用户，且土耳其语缺乏高质量的回合转换数据集，因此需要构建适用于该语言的合成数据集以改善对话时序管理。

Method: 使用多种Qwen大语言模型生成模拟真实对话中重叠和策略性沉默现象的土耳其语合成对话数据集Syn-TurnTurk，并采用传统机器学习与深度学习模型（如BI-LSTM和LR+RF集成方法）进行评估。

Result: BI-LSTM和集成模型（LR+RF）在该数据集上表现最佳，准确率达到0.839，AUC分数达到0.910，表明合成数据能有效提升模型对语言线索的理解。

Conclusion: Syn-TurnTurk数据集有助于提升土耳其语语音对话系统中的回合预测性能，推动更自然的人机交互体验。

Abstract: Managing natural dialogue timing is a significant challenge for voice-based chatbots. Most current systems usually rely on simple silence detection, which often fails because human speech patterns involve irregular pauses. This causes bots to interrupt users, breaking the conversational flow. This problem is even more severe for languages like Turkish, which lack high-quality datasets for turn-taking prediction. This paper introduces Syn-TurnTurk, a synthetic Turkish dialogue dataset generated using various Qwen Large Language Models (LLMs) to mirror real-life verbal exchanges, including overlaps and strategic silences. We evaluated the dataset using several traditional and deep learning architectures. The results show that advanced models, particularly BI-LSTM and Ensemble (LR+RF) methods, achieve high accuracy (0.839) and AUC scores (0.910). These findings demonstrate that our synthetic dataset can have a positive affect for models understand linguistic cues, allowing for more natural human-machine interaction in Turkish.

</details>


### [119] [Calibrated Speculative Decoding: Frequency-Guided Candidate Selection for Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13634)
*Xuwen Zhou,Fangxin Liu,Chao Wang,Xiao Zheng,Hao Zheng,Min He,Li Jiang,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的推测解码框架Calibrated Speculative Decoding (CSD)，通过频率引导候选选择和概率保护接受机制，有效恢复被传统方法错误拒绝的有效令牌，显著提升大语言模型生成速度与复杂推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推测解码因严格匹配机制频繁误拒语义正确但词法不同的草案令牌，导致效率损失，本文旨在解决这一问题。

Method: 提出CSD框架，包含两个轻量模块：在线修正记忆（Online Correction Memory）用于收集历史拒绝模式并提供救援候选；语义一致性门控（Semantic Consistency Gating）利用概率比而非精确匹配来验证候选令牌。

Result: 在多种大语言模型上实验表明，CSD实现最高2.33倍吞吐量加速，在保持模型准确率的同时，进一步提升了复杂推理任务的表现。

Conclusion: CSD是一种高效、轻量且无需训练的推测解码方案，适用于实际的大语言模型部署场景。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by letting draft tokens bypass full verification, but conventional frameworks suffer from frequent false rejections, particularly when draft models produce semantically correct but lexically divergent outputs. In this paper, we present Calibrated Speculative Decoding (CSD), a training-free framework that recovers valid tokens discarded by standard verification. Guided by the principle of "Frequency-Guided Candidate Selection and Probability-Guarded Acceptance," CSD incorporates two lightweight modules: Online Correction Memory, which aggregates historical rejections to propose recurring divergence patterns as rescue candidates, and Semantic Consistency Gating, which verifies candidate admissibility using probability ratios instead of exact token matching. Our evaluation across diverse large language models demonstrates that CSD outperforms existing methods, achieving a peak throughput speedup of 2.33x. CSD preserves model accuracy across all tasks while further boosting performance on complex reasoning datasets. These results establish CSD as a highly effective, lightweight solution for practical LLM deployments.

</details>


### [120] [Breaking the Generator Barrier: Disentangled Representation for Generalizable AI-Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13692)
*Xiao Pu,Zepeng Cheng,Lin Yuan,Yu Wu,Xiuli Bi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种渐进式结构化框架，用于增强AI生成文本检测的泛化能力，通过解耦语义与生成器相关特征，在多模型场景下实现了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本检测方法依赖特定生成器的特征，难以泛化到未见模型，亟需一种对新型大模型具有鲁棒性的检测机制。

Method: 设计了一个包含紧凑潜在编码、基于扰动的正则化和判别性适配阶段的渐进式框架，以分离AI检测语义与生成器相关伪影。

Result: 在涵盖20个LLM的MAGE基准上，相比现有最优方法最高提升了24.2%准确率和26.2% F1值，且随训练生成器多样性增加性能持续提升。

Conclusion: 该框架有效增强了AI文本检测在开放集场景下的可扩展性和泛化能力，为应对不断演进的生成模型提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) generate text that increasingly resembles human writing, the subtle cues that distinguish AI-generated content from human-written content become increasingly challenging to capture. Reliance on generator-specific artifacts is inherently unstable, since new models emerge rapidly and reduce the robustness of such shortcuts. This generalizes unseen generators as a central and challenging problem for AI-text detection. To tackle this challenge, we propose a progressively structured framework that disentangles AI-detection semantics from generator-aware artifacts. This is achieved through a compact latent encoding that encourages semantic minimality, followed by perturbation-based regularization to reduce residual entanglement, and finally a discriminative adaptation stage that aligns representations with task objectives. Experiments on MAGE benchmark, covering 20 representative LLMs across 7 categories, demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods, achieving up to 24.2% accuracy gain and 26.2% F1 improvement. Notably, performance continues to improve as the diversity of training generators increases, confirming strong scalability and generalization in open-set scenarios. Our source code will be publicly available at https://github.com/PuXiao06/DRGD.

</details>


### [121] [Beyond Arrow's Impossibility: Fairness as an Emergent Property of Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13705)
*Sayan Kumar Chaki,Antoine Gourru,Julien Velcin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出公平性并非单个语言模型的属性，而是通过多智能体交互与协商过程中涌现的特性。研究通过一个医院分诊的三轮辩论框架，探讨对齐与未对齐智能体之间的谈判如何共同达成比任一单独智能体更公平的决策结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型日益具备自主性，传统的以单一中心化模型为单位研究公平性的方法已不足够，需转向多智能体互动中的程序性公平。

Method: 构建一个受控的医院分诊场景，两个智能体进行三轮结构化辩论；其中一个通过检索增强生成（RAG）对齐特定伦理框架，另一个则未对齐或被引导偏向人口统计特征而非临床需求。

Result: 对齐影响了谈判策略和资源分配模式；单独来看任一智能体的分配都不够公平，但最终联合分配可满足单一智能体无法达到的公平标准；对齐智能体通过对抗而非压制部分纠正偏见，但仍存在内在倾向性偏差。

Conclusion: 公平性应被视为去中心化智能体交互中涌现的过程性属性，评估单位应从个体智能体转向整个系统。

Abstract: Fairness in language models is typically studied as a property of a single, centrally optimized model. As large language models become increasingly agentic, we propose that fairness emerges through interaction and exchange. We study this via a controlled hospital triage framework in which two agents negotiate over three structured debate rounds. One agent is aligned to a specific ethical framework via retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), while the other is either unaligned or adversarially prompted to favor demographic groups over clinical need. We find that alignment systematically shapes negotiation strategies and allocation patterns, and that neither agent's allocation is ethically adequate in isolation, yet their joint final allocation can satisfy fairness criteria that neither would have reached alone. Aligned agents partially moderate bias through contestation rather than override, acting as corrective patches that restore access for marginalized groups without fully converting a biased counterpart. We further observe that even explicitly aligned agents exhibit intrinsic biases toward certain frameworks, consistent with known left-leaning tendencies in LLMs. We connect these limits to Arrow's Impossibility Theorem: no aggregation mechanism can simultaneously satisfy all desiderata of collective rationality, and multi-agent deliberation navigates rather than resolves this constraint. Our results reposition fairness as an emergent, procedural property of decentralized agent interaction, and the system rather than the individual agent as the appropriate unit of evaluation.

</details>


### [122] [Co-FactChecker: A Framework for Human-AI Collaborative Claim Verification Using Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13706)
*Dhruv Sahnan,Subhabrata Dutta,Tanmoy Chakraborty,Preslav Nakov,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Co-FactChecker是一个人机协作的声明验证框架，通过将专家反馈转化为对模型思维轨迹的编辑，提升验证效果和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型缺乏领域知识和上下文理解，与专业事实核查存在差距，需通过人机协作弥补。

Method: 提出Co-FactChecker框架，将模型的思考过程作为共享草稿区，把专家反馈转化为对推理轨迹的直接编辑，并在理论和实验上验证其优势。

Result: 自动评估显示Co-FactChecker优于现有自主和协作方法，人工评估表明其推理质量更高、结论更可靠、思维轨迹更易理解。

Conclusion: 基于思维轨迹编辑的人机协作模式在声明验证中比传统对话式交互更有效，是提升模型推理能力的可行路径。

Abstract: Professional fact-checkers rely on domain knowledge and deep contextual understanding to verify claims. Large language models (LLMs) and large reasoning models (LRMs) lack such grounding and primarily reason from available evidence alone, creating a mismatch between expert-led and fully automated claim verification. To mitigate this gap, we posit human-AI collaboration as a more promising path forward, where expert feedback, grounded in real-world knowledge and domain expertise, guides the model's reasoning. However, existing LRMs are hard to calibrate to natural language feedback, particularly in a multi-turn interaction setup. We propose Co-FactChecker, a framework for human-AI collaborative claim verification. We introduce a new interaction paradigm that treats the model's thinking trace as a shared scratchpad. Co-FactChecker translates expert feedback into trace-edits that introduce targeted modifications to the trace, sidestepping the shortcomings of dialogue-based interaction. We provide theoretical results showing that trace-editing offers advantages over multi-turn dialogue, and our automatic evaluations demonstrate that Co-FactChecker outperforms existing autonomous and human-AI collaboration approaches. Human evaluations further show that Co-FactChecker is preferred over multi-turn dialogue, producing higher quality reasoning and verdicts along with relatively easier to interpret and more useful thinking traces.

</details>


### [123] [Learning the Cue or Learning the Word? Analyzing Generalization in Metaphor Detection for Verbs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13713)
*Sinan Kurtyigit,Sabine Schulte im Walde,Alexander Fraser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过RoBERTa模型分析隐喻检测中的泛化能力，采用控制词汇保留设置评估词元级别的泛化表现，发现上下文模式驱动主要的可迁移泛化，而词汇记忆仅提供额外增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 明确当前隐喻检测模型的高性能是源于可迁移的泛化能力还是词汇记忆，厘清模型学习机制的本质。

Method: 使用RoBERTa模型，在VU阿姆斯特丹隐喻语料库上对英语动词进行分析，设计严格的词汇保留设置，将目标词元完全排除在微调之外，并比较模型对保留词元和暴露词元的预测性能；进一步分析句子上下文和静态词向量的作用。

Result: 模型在暴露词元上表现最佳，但在保留词元上仍保持稳健性能；仅利用句子上下文即可达到完整模型在保留词元上的性能水平，而静态动词级嵌入无法做到这一点。

Conclusion: 隐喻检测中的泛化主要由可迁移的上下文模式（即‘学习线索’）驱动，而‘学习词语’（特定动词的记忆）仅在有词汇接触时提供附加提升。

Abstract: Metaphor detection models achieve strong benchmark performance, yet it remains unclear whether this reflects transferable generalization or lexical memorization. To address this, we analyze generalization in metaphor detection through RoBERTa, the shared backbone of many state-of-the-art systems, focusing on English verbs using the VU Amsterdam Metaphor Corpus. We introduce a controlled lexical hold-out setup where all instances of selected target lemmas are strictly excluded from fine-tuning, and compare predictions on these Held-out lemmas against Exposed lemmas (verbs seen during fine-tuning). While the model performs best on Exposed lemmas, it maintains robust performance on Held-out lemmas. Further analysis reveals that sentence context alone is sufficient to match full-model performance on Held-out lemmas, whereas static verb-level embeddings are not. Together, these results suggest that generalization is primarily driven by "learning the cue" (transferable contextual patterns), while "learning the word" (verb-specific memorization) provides an additive boost when lexical exposure is available.

</details>


### [124] [An Empirical Investigation of Practical LLM-as-a-Judge Improvement Techniques on RewardBench 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13717)
*Ryan Lail*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了提升GPT-5.4作为评判模型准确性的实用技术，发现任务特定标准注入和集成评分效果显著，结合使用可将准确率提升11.9个百分点至83.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高LLM-as-a-judge在无需微调情况下的评判可靠性，解决其对提示和聚合策略依赖性强的问题。

Method: 通过在RewardBench 2上进行实证研究，评估多种即插即用技术对GPT-5.4评判准确率的影响，重点分析任务特定标准注入、集成评分及其他三种辅助方法。

Result: 任务特定标准注入提升+3.0pp（成本极低），集成评分提升+9.8pp（成本5倍）；两者结合达到83.6%准确率，比基线71.7%提升11.9pp；较小模型通过集成也能以低成本实现高准确率（如GPT-5.4 mini k=8达79.2%）。

Conclusion: 任务特定标准注入与集成评分是提升LLM评判准确率最有效的无训练方法，且小模型通过集成可在低成本下实现高性价比的评判性能。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge, using a language model to score or rank candidate responses, is widely used as a scalable alternative to human evaluation in RLHF pipelines, benchmarking, and application layer evaluations (evals). However, judgment reliability depends heavily on prompting and aggregation strategy. We present an empirical investigation of practical, drop-in techniques that improve GPT-5.4 judge accuracy on RewardBench 2 without any finetuning. Two techniques account for nearly all available gains: task-specific criteria injection (+3.0pp at negligible cost) and ensemble scoring (+9.8pp at 5x cost). Combined, they reach 83.6% accuracy, +11.9pp over the 71.7% baseline. Our investigation also covers three further techniques (calibration context, adaptive model escalation, and soft blending) which did not reliably improve on criteria + ensembling at comparable cost. Cheaper model tiers benefit disproportionately from ensembling: GPT-5.4 mini with k=8 achieves 79.2% at 1.2x baseline cost, and GPT-5.4 nano with k=8 reaches 71.4% at 0.4x baseline cost, making high-accuracy LLM judges accessible at low cost.

</details>


### [125] [MedRCube: A Multidimensional Framework for Fine-Grained and In-Depth Evaluation of MLLMs in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13756)
*Zhijie Bao,Fangke Chen,Licheng Bao,Chenhui Zhang,Wei Chen,Jiajie Peng,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多维度、细粒度的评估范式MedRCube，用于系统评估多模态大语言模型在医学影像中的表现，并揭示了现有模型在推理可信度和捷径行为方面的关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医学影像评估方法多依赖单一或粗粒度指标，缺乏对临床实际所需的细粒度分析能力与推理可靠性的深入评估，因此需要一种更系统、严谨的评估框架。

Method: 提出一种两阶段系统化构建流程，实现多维、细粒度、深度评估，并基于此构建MedRCube基准；在33个MLLM上进行评测，引入可信推理子集以量化推理的可信度。

Result: Lingshu-32B在33个模型中表现最佳；MedRCube揭示了以往评估无法发现的关键洞察；发现捷径行为与诊断任务性能之间存在显著正相关，提示潜在可靠性风险。

Conclusion: MedRCube推动了医学多模态模型评估的范式转变，强调细粒度与可信评估的重要性，为临床可信部署提供了新的评估标准与资源支持。

Abstract: The potential of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) in domain of medical imaging raise the demands of systematic and rigorous evaluation frameworks that are aligned with the real-world medical imaging practice. Existing practices that report single or coarse-grained metrics are lack the granularity required for specialized clinical support and fail to assess the reliability of reasoning mechanisms. To address this, we propose a paradigm shift toward multidimensional, fine-grained and in-depth evaluation. Based on a two-stage systematic construction pipeline designed for this paradigm, we instantiate it with MedRCube. We benchmark 33 MLLMs, \textit{Lingshu-32B} achieve top-tier performance. Crucially, MedRCube exposes a series of pronounced insights inaccessible under prior evaluation settings. Furthermore, we introduce a credibility evaluation subset to quantify reasoning credibility, uncover a highly significant positive association between shortcut behavior and diagnostic task performance, raising concerns for clinically trustworthy deployment. The resources of this work can be found at https://github.com/F1mc/MedRCube.

</details>


### [126] [From Anchors to Supervision: Memory-Graph Guided Corpus-Free Unlearning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13777)
*Wenxuan Li,Zhenfei Zhang,Mi Zhang,Geng Hong,Mi Wen,Xiaoyu You,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MAGE是一种基于记忆图引导的、用户最小化且无需语料库的大语言模型遗忘框架，通过轻量级锚点识别目标实体并自动生成监督信息，实现可审计、安全且高效的知识擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可能记忆敏感或受版权保护的内容，现有机器遗忘方法依赖用户提供的遗忘集，存在难以审计、二次泄露和恶意滥用的风险。因此需要一种更安全、可验证的遗忘机制。

Method: 提出MAGE框架：利用轻量级用户锚点探测目标大模型以恢复相关记忆内容，构建加权局部记忆图，并据此生成范围明确的监督信号用于知识遗忘；该方法不依赖原始训练语料，具有模型无关性，可集成到标准遗忘方法中。

Result: 在TOFU和RWKU两个基准上的实验表明，MAGE自生成的监督信号在遗忘效果上可媲美外部参考生成的监督，同时有效保持模型整体效用。

Conclusion: MAGE支持仅依赖最小锚点的实用且可审计的遗忘流程，避免了用户提交遗忘语料带来的风险，为大模型的安全与合规应用提供了新路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) may memorize sensitive or copyrighted content, raising significant privacy and legal concerns. While machine unlearning has emerged as a potential remedy, prevailing paradigms rely on user-provided forget sets, making unlearning requests difficult to audit and exposing systems to secondary leakage and malicious abuse. We propose MAGE, a Memory-grAph Guided Erasure framework for user-minimized, corpus-free unlearning. Given only a lightweight user anchor that identifies a target entity, MAGE probes the target LLM to recover target-related memorization, organizes it into a weighted local memory graph, and synthesizes scoped supervision for unlearning. MAGE is model-agnostic, can be plugged into standard unlearning methods, and requires no access to the original training corpus. Experiments on two benchmarks, TOFU and RWKU, demonstrate that MAGE's self-generated supervision achieves effective unlearning performance comparable to supervision generated with external reference, while preserving overall utility. These results support a practical and auditable unlearning workflow driven by minimal anchors rather than user-supplied forget corpora.

</details>


### [127] [QuantileMark: A Message-Symmetric Multi-bit Watermark for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13786)
*Junlin Zhu,Baizhou Huang,Xiaojun Wan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为QuantileMark的白盒多比特水印方法，通过在连续累积概率区间[0,1)内嵌入消息，确保每个消息符号分配到等质量的区间，从而实现生成质量和检测性能的消息对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于词汇划分的水印方法在低熵解码时会破坏消息对称性，导致不同消息在生成质量和检测准确率上表现不一致，因此需要一种能够保证消息对称性的新方法。

Method: QuantileMark将累积概率区间[0,1)划分为M个等质量的子区间，每个目标符号对应一个子区间，在生成时仅从对应子区间中采样；验证时通过教师强制重建分区并聚合潜在区间的后验证据进行检测。

Result: 理论证明了该方法具有消息无偏性，实验结果表明其在C4和LFQA数据集上相比强基线方法具有更好的多比特恢复能力和检测鲁棒性，且对生成质量影响可忽略。

Conclusion: QuantileMark提供了一种理论上支持生成侧对称性和检测侧均匀证据强度的多比特水印方案，适用于大语言模型的内容溯源需求。

Abstract: As large language models become standard backends for content generation, practical provenance increasingly requires multi-bit watermarking. In provider-internal deployments, a key requirement is message symmetry: the message itself should not systematically affect either text quality or verification outcomes. Vocabulary-partition watermarks can break message symmetry in low-entropy decoding: some messages are assigned most of the probability mass, while others are forced to use tail tokens. This makes embedding quality and message decoding accuracy message-dependent. We propose QuantileMark, a white-box multi-bit watermark that embeds messages within the continuous cumulative probability interval $[0, 1)$. At each step, QuantileMark partitions this interval into $M$ equal-mass bins and samples strictly from the bin assigned to the target symbol, ensuring a fixed $1/M$ probability budget regardless of context entropy. For detection, the verifier reconstructs the same partition under teacher forcing, computes posteriors over latent bins, and aggregates evidence for verification. We prove message-unbiasedness, a property ensuring that the base distribution is recovered when averaging over messages. This provides a theoretical foundation for generation-side symmetry, while the equal-mass design additionally promotes uniform evidence strength across messages on the detection side. Empirical results on C4 continuation and LFQA show improved multi-bit recovery and detection robustness over strong baselines, with negligible impact on generation quality. Our code is available at GitHub (https://github.com/zzzjunlin/QuantileMark).

</details>


### [128] [MUSE: Multi-Domain Chinese User Simulation via Self-Evolving Profiles and Rubric-Guided Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13828)
*Zihao Liu,Hantao Zhou,Jiguo Li,Jun Xu,Jiuchong Gao,Jinghua Hao,Renqing He,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MUSE，一种多领域中文用户模拟框架，通过迭代自优化用户画像、角色反转微调和基于评分标准的强化学习，实现更真实、连贯且具人格一致性的对话模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户模拟方法在中文支持、跨领域能力及长期交互中的人设一致性方面存在不足，难以满足可扩展的AI系统训练与评估需求。

Method: 提出MUSE框架：1）采用迭代画像自进化（IPSE）优化用户画像；2）应用角色反转监督微调提升局部回复真实性；3）构建评分标准奖励模型并结合多轮强化学习实现行为对齐。

Result: 实验表明，MUSE在话语级和会话级评估中均优于强基线模型，生成的响应在长期交互中更具真实性、连贯性和人设一致性。

Conclusion: MUSE有效提升了中文用户模拟在多领域、长周期交互中的表现，为交互式AI系统的训练与评估提供了更可靠的模拟环境。

Abstract: User simulators are essential for the scalable training and evaluation of interactive AI systems. However, existing approaches often rely on shallow user profiling, struggle to maintain persona consistency over long interactions, and are largely limited to English or single-domain settings. We present MUSE, a multi-domain Chinese user simulation framework designed to generate human-like, controllable, and behaviorally consistent responses. First, we propose Iterative Profile Self-Evolution (IPSE), which gradually optimizes user profiles by comparing and reasoning discrepancies between simulated trajectories and real dialogue behaviors. We then apply Role-Reversal Supervised Fine-Tuning to improve local response realism and human-like expression. To enable fine-grained behavioral alignment, we further train a specialized rubric-based reward model and incorporate it into rubric-guided multi-turn reinforcement learning, which optimizes the simulator at the dialogue level and enhances long-horizon behavioral consistency. Experiments show that MUSE consistently outperforms strong baselines in both utterance-level and session-level evaluations, generating responses that are more realistic, coherent, and persona-consistent over extended interactions.

</details>


### [129] [Robust Reward Modeling for Large Language Models via Causal Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13833)
*Yunsheng Lu,Zijiang Yang,Licheng Pan,Zhixuan Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于意图重建的正则化方法，通过解码器将候选回答映射到输入的潜在意图嵌入，利用重建误差来增强奖励模型对提示意图的依赖，抑制长度和谄媚等捷径效应，在多个基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励模型容易过拟合于响应长度、谄媚语调等表面线索，缺乏对提示真实意图的依赖，导致评估偏差。

Method: 设计一个解码器，将候选回答映射回输入的潜在意图嵌入，利用其重建误差作为正则化信号加入奖励模型训练，理论上该信号能强化与提示相关的信息并抑制无关捷径。

Result: 在数学、有益性和安全性基准上，解码器以0.877准确率选出更短、更少谄媚的回答；应用于Gemma-2系列模型后，RewardBench准确率从0.832提升至0.868；Best-of-N选择中提升了长度控制下的胜率，输出更短且对改写更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过意图重建正则化可有效引导奖励模型关注提示意图，缓解对表面特征的依赖，提升对齐效果和生成质量。

Abstract: Reward models are central to aligning large language models, yet they often overfit to spurious cues such as response length and overly agreeable tone. Most prior work weakens these cues directly by penalizing or controlling specific artifacts, but it does not explicitly encourage the model to ground preferences in the prompt's intent. We learn a decoder that maps a candidate answer to the latent intent embedding of the input. The reconstruction error is used as a signal to regularize the reward model training. We provide theoretical evidence that this signal emphasizes prompt-dependent information while suppressing prompt-independent shortcuts. Across math, helpfulness, and safety benchmarks, the decoder selects shorter and less sycophantic candidates with 0.877 accuracy. Incorporating this signal into RM training in Gemma-2-2B-it and Gemma-2-9B-it increases RewardBench accuracy from 0.832 to 0.868. For Best-of-N selection, our framework increases length-controlled win rates while producing shorter outputs, and remains robust to lengthening and mild off-topic drift in controlled rewrite tests.

</details>


### [130] [Beyond Static Personas: Situational Personality Steering for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13846)
*Zesheng Wei,Mengxiang Li,Zilei Wang,Yang Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练、基于神经元的框架IRIS，用于实现大语言模型在不同情境下的个性化控制，通过识别、检索和加权引导情境相关神经元，提升了个性化的可控性与适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化大语言模型方法存在可控性差、资源消耗高以及依赖静态人格建模导致难以适应不同情境的问题，因此需要一种更灵活、高效的情境感知个性化方法。

Method: 首先通过多视角分析揭示大模型人格神经元中存在情境依赖性和稳定的行为模式；然后提出IRIS框架，包括情境人格神经元识别、情境感知的神经元检索和相似性加权引导三个步骤，实现无需训练的动态人格调控。

Result: 在PersonalityBench和新构建的SPBench上进行实验验证，结果表明IRIS优于现有最优基线方法，具备良好的泛化能力和鲁棒性，能有效应对复杂且未见过的情境及不同的模型架构。

Conclusion: IRIS为个性化大语言模型提供了一种高效、灵活且无需训练的情境化人格控制方案，推动了更具适应性和可控性的人机交互发展。

Abstract: Personalized Large Language Models (LLMs) facilitate more natural, human-like interactions in human-centric applications. However, existing personalization methods are constrained by limited controllability and high resource demands. Furthermore, their reliance on static personality modeling restricts adaptability across varying situations. To address these limitations, we first demonstrate the existence of situation-dependency and consistent situation-behavior patterns within LLM personalities through a multi-perspective analysis of persona neurons. Building on these insights, we propose IRIS, a training-free, neuron-based Identify-Retrieve-Steer framework for advanced situational personality steering. Our approach comprises situational persona neuron identification, situation-aware neuron retrieval, and similarity-weighted steering. We empirically validate our framework on PersonalityBench and our newly introduced SPBench, a comprehensive situational personality benchmark. Experimental results show that our method surpasses best-performing baselines, demonstrating IRIS's generalization and robustness to complex, unseen situations and different models architecture.

</details>


### [131] [Do We Still Need Humans in the Loop? Comparing Human and LLM Annotation in Active Learning for Hostility Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13899)
*Ahmad Dawar Hakimi,Lea Hirlimann,Isabelle Augenstein,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了指令调优的语言模型（LLM）在主动学习（AL）中替代人工标注的可行性，发现在检测德国政治类TikTok评论中的反移民敌意任务中，使用大量低成本LLM标注数据（如GPT-5.2）训练的分类器性能接近于使用较少人工标注数据训练的分类器，但LLM标注导致系统性误判，尤其在话题模糊、敌意与政策批评界限不清的情况下。因此，选择标注策略应基于目标应用可接受的错误类型，而非仅看总体F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在主动学习框架中是否可用语言模型（LLM）生成的标签替代人工标注，并评估当整个语料库都能以极低成本标注时，主动学习是否仍有价值。

Method: 在一个包含277,902条德语政治类TikTok评论的新数据集上，比较七种标注策略和四种编码器，分析LLM标注（GPT-5.2）与人工标注在检测反移民敌意任务中的表现差异。

Result: 使用25,974个LLM标注样本训练的分类器（成本\$43）在F1-Macro上与使用3,800个人工标注样本训练的分类器（成本\$316）性能相当；主动学习在预富集的数据池中相比随机采样无明显优势，且在相同成本下F1低于全LLM标注；但LLM训练的模型存在系统性偏差，倾向于高估正类，尤其是在语义模糊的评论中。

Conclusion: 尽管LLM标注在整体指标上可媲美人工标注，但其错误模式不同，尤其在敏感语境下可能产生误导；因此，标注策略的选择应结合具体应用场景对错误类型的容忍度，而不仅仅依赖于聚合评估指标如F1。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned LLMs can annotate thousands of instances from a short prompt at negligible cost. This raises two questions for active learning (AL): can LLM labels replace human labels within the AL loop, and does AL remain necessary when entire corpora can be labelled at once? We investigate both questions on a new dataset of 277,902 German political TikTok comments (25,974 LLM-labelled, 5,000 human-annotated), comparing seven annotation strategies across four encoders to detect anti-immigrant hostility. A classifier trained on 25,974 GPT-5.2 labels (\$43) achieves comparable F1-Macro to one trained on 3,800 human annotations (\$316). Active learning offers little advantage over random sampling in our pre-enriched pool and delivers lower F1 than full LLM annotation at the same cost. However, comparable aggregate F1 masks a systematic difference in error structure: LLM-trained classifiers over-predict the positive class relative to the human gold standard. This divergence concentrates in topically ambiguous discussions where the distinction between anti-immigrant hostility and policy critique is most subtle, suggesting that annotation strategy should be guided not by aggregate F1 alone but by the error profile acceptable for the target application.

</details>


### [132] [Causal Drawbridges: Characterizing Gradient Blocking of Syntactic Islands in Transformer LMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13950)
*Sasha Boguraev,Kyle Mahowald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过因果干预Transformer模型，研究揭示了英语句法中协调性孤岛现象的机制，并提出‘and’在可提取与不可提取结构中表征不同，推动了对句法表征的新理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决句法理论中长期存在的难题——句法孤岛现象，特别是动词短语协调结构中的提取问题，并探究Transformer模型是否能模拟人类对此类结构的接受度梯度变化。

Method: 在Transformer模型中使用因果干预方法，隔离与功能相关的子空间（如注意力模块和MLP），分析其在处理wh-依赖和协调孤岛时的机制；并将大量无关文本投射到这些因果识别出的子空间以推导语言学假设。

Result: Transformer模型能够复现人类对协调孤岛结构接受度的梯度判断；提取机制与标准wh-依赖相同，但在不同程度上被选择性阻断；发现‘and’在可提取与不可提取结构中具有不同的表征，对应于关系依赖表达与纯并列用法的区别。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的机械可解释性方法不仅能揭示句法处理的内在机制，还能生成关于语言表征的新语言学假说，促进句法理论的发展。

Abstract: We show how causal interventions in Transformer models provide insights into English syntax by focusing on a long-standing challenge for syntactic theory: syntactic islands. Extraction from coordinated verb phrases is often degraded, yet acceptability varies gradiently with lexical content (e.g., "I know what he hates art and loves" vs. "I know what he looked down and saw"). We show that modern Transformer language models replicate human judgments across this gradient. Using causal interventions that isolate functionally relevant subspaces in Transformer blocks, attention modules, and MLPs, we demonstrate that extraction from coordination islands engages the same filler-gap mechanisms as canonical wh-dependencies, but that these mechanisms are selectively blocked to varying degrees. By projecting a large corpus of unrelated text onto these causally identified subspaces, we derive a novel linguistic hypothesis: the conjunction "and" is represented differently in extractable versus non-extractable constructions, corresponding to expressions encoding relational dependencies versus purely conjunctive uses. These results illustrate how mechanistic interpretability can inform syntax, generating new hypotheses about linguistic representation and processing.

</details>


### [133] [Adaptive Conformal Prediction for Improving Factuality of Generations by Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13991)
*Aleksandr Rubashevskii,Dzianis Piatrashyn,Preslav Nakov,Maxim Panov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应的保校准方法，用于提升大语言模型生成内容的真实性不确定性估计，支持输入依赖的校准和选择性预测，在多个白盒模型上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保校准方法缺乏对提示词依赖的变异性建模，导致覆盖不足或过度覆盖，难以有效控制大语言模型生成事实错误的问题。

Method: 扩展保校准分数变换方法至大语言模型，引入提示词自适应机制，实现输入相关的校准，并支持选择性预测以过滤不可靠输出。

Result: 在长文本生成和多项选择问答任务中验证了方法有效性，显著提升了条件覆盖率，同时保持边际覆盖率的统计保证。

Conclusion: 该方法通过提示自适应的保校准策略，改善了大语言模型在不同输入下的不确定性估计，增强了生成结果的可信度和实用性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are prone to generating factually incorrect outputs. Recent work has applied conformal prediction to provide uncertainty estimates and statistical guarantees for the factuality of LLM generations. However, existing approaches are typically not prompt-adaptive, limiting their ability to capture input-dependent variability. As a result, they may filter out too few items (leading to over-coverage) or too many (under-coverage) for a given task or prompt. We propose an adaptive conformal prediction approach that extends conformal score transformation methods to LLMs, with applications to long-form generation and multiple-choice question answering. This enables prompt-dependent calibration, retaining marginal coverage guarantees while improving conditional coverage. In addition, the approach naturally supports selective prediction, allowing unreliable claims or answer choices to be filtered out in downstream applications. We evaluate our approach on multiple white-box models across diverse domains and show that it significantly outperforms existing baselines in terms of conditional coverage.

</details>


### [134] [Diffusion Language Models for Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14001)
*Davyd Naveriani,Albert Zeyer,Ralf Schlüter,Hermann Ney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）和统一状态扩散模型（USDM）应用于语音识别中的重排序，并提出了一种结合CTC与USDM的联合解码方法，显著提升了识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索扩散语言模型在语音识别中的潜力，尤其是其双向注意力和并行生成能力，以提升ASR系统的性能。

Method: 引入MDLM和USDM用于ASR假设重排序，并设计一种新的联合解码方法，融合CTC的帧级概率分布和USDM的标签级概率分布。

Result: 实验结果表明，USDM和MDLM均能显著提高文本识别的准确性。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型是改进语音识别系统性能的有效工具，所提出的联合解码方法有效结合了声学和语言信息。

Abstract: Diffusion language models have recently emerged as a leading alternative to standard language models, due to their ability for bidirectional attention and parallel text generation. In this work, we explore variants for their use in speech recognition. Specifically, we introduce a comprehensive guide to incorporating masked diffusion language models (MDLM) and uniform-state diffusion models (USDMs) for rescoring ASR hypotheses. Additionally, we design a new joint-decoding method that combines CTC and USDM by integrating the framewise probability distributions derived from CTC with the labelwise probability distributions computed by USDM at each decoding step, thereby generating new candidates that combine strong language knowledge from USDM and acoustic information from CTC. Our findings reveal that USDM, as well as MDLM, can significantly improve the accuracy of recognized text. We publish all our code and recipes.

</details>


### [135] [Dual-Enhancement Product Bundling: Bridging Interactive Graph and Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14030)
*Zhe Huang,Peng Wang,Yan Zheng,Sen Song,Longjun Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合图学习与大语言模型的双增强方法，通过动态概念绑定机制将图结构转化为自然语言提示，有效提升电商场景下的商品捆绑推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同过滤方法难以处理冷启动商品，而大语言模型无法直接建模交互图结构，导致在商品捆绑推荐中表现受限。

Method: 提出图到文本的范式，引入动态概念绑定机制（DCBM），将图结构信息转化为适合大语言模型理解的自然语言提示，并融合图学习与语义理解进行商品捆绑推荐。

Result: 在POG、POG_dense和Steam三个基准上实验表明，该方法比现有最优基线提升了6.3%-26.5%。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了冷启动商品和图结构与大语言模型融合的难题，显著提升了商品捆绑推荐的效果。

Abstract: Product bundling boosts e-commerce revenue by recommending complementary item combinations. However, existing methods face two critical challenges: (1) collaborative filtering approaches struggle with cold-start items owing to dependency on historical interactions, and (2) LLMs lack inherent capability to model interactive graph directly. To bridge this gap, we propose a dual-enhancement method that integrates interactive graph learning and LLM-based semantic understanding for product bundling. Our method introduces a graph-to-text paradigm, which leverages a Dynamic Concept Binding Mechanism (DCBM) to translate graph structures into natural language prompts. The DCBM plays a critical role in aligning domain-specific entities with LLM tokenization, enabling effective comprehension of combinatorial constraints. Experiments on three benchmarks (POG, POG_dense, Steam) demonstrate 6.3%-26.5% improvements over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [136] [From Where Words Come: Efficient Regularization of Code Tokenizers Through Source Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14053)
*Pavel Chizhov,Egor Bogomolov,Ivan P. Yamshchikov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了代码分词的效率问题，提出了一种名为源属性BPE（SA-BPE）的新方法，通过修改BPE目标和引入合并跳过技术，减少未训练充分的标记数量，同时保持与常规BPE相同的推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于训练数据中存储库和语言多样性不平衡以及源特定重复标记占主导地位，导致代码分词器容易产生未使用且因此欠训练的标记，影响LLM的效率和安全性。

Method: 通过修改BPE目标函数并引入合并跳过机制，提出SA-BPE方法来规范化BPE训练过程，减少过拟合现象。

Result: SA-BPE显著减少了欠训练标记的数量，同时维持了与标准BPE一致的推理流程。

Conclusion: SA-BPE是一种有效的、适用于生产的工具，能够提升代码分词的质量，从而增强大语言模型的效率和安全性。

Abstract: Efficiency and safety of Large Language Models (LLMs), among other factors, rely on the quality of tokenization. A good tokenizer not only improves inference speed and language understanding but also provides extra defense against jailbreak attacks and lowers the risk of hallucinations. In this work, we investigate the efficiency of code tokenization, in particular from the perspective of data source diversity. We demonstrate that code tokenizers are prone to producing unused, and thus under-trained, tokens due to the imbalance in repository and language diversity in the training data, as well as the dominance of source-specific, repetitive tokens that are often unusable in future inference. By modifying the BPE objective and introducing merge skipping, we implement different techniques under the name Source-Attributed BPE (SA-BPE) to regularize BPE training and minimize overfitting, thereby substantially reducing the number of under-trained tokens while maintaining the same inference procedure as with regular BPE. This provides an effective tool suitable for production use.

</details>


### [137] [From Weights to Activations: Is Steering the Next Frontier of Adaptation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14090)
*Simon Ostermann,Daniil Gurgurov,Tanja Baeumel,Michael A. Hedderich,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Wojciech Samek,Vera Schmitt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将语言模型的steering视为一种新型的模型适应范式，通过在激活空间中进行定向干预，实现无需参数更新的局部且可逆的行为调整，并提出了统一的模型适应分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管steering在实践中被广泛使用，但其很少与传统的模型适应方法在同一个理论框架下进行分析，因此需要建立统一的理解框架。

Method: 提出一组用于评估适应方法的功能性标准，并利用这些标准将steering方法与传统方法进行系统比较。

Result: 分析表明，steering是一种基于激活空间中定向干预的独特适应范式，能够实现局部、可逆且无需参数更新的行为调整。

Conclusion: steering应被视为一种与传统方法并列的模型适应形式，该视角有助于构建涵盖steering与传统方法的统一模型适应分类体系。

Abstract: Post-training adaptation of language models is commonly achieved through parameter updates or input-based methods such as fine-tuning, parameter-efficient adaptation, and prompting. In parallel, a growing body of work modifies internal activations at inference time to influence model behavior, an approach known as steering. Despite increasing use, steering is rarely analyzed within the same conceptual framework as established adaptation methods.
  In this work, we argue that steering should be regarded as a form of model adaptation. We introduce a set of functional criteria for adaptation methods and use them to compare steering approaches with classical alternatives. This analysis positions steering as a distinct adaptation paradigm based on targeted interventions in activation space, enabling local and reversible behavioral change without parameter updates. The resulting framing clarifies how steering relates to existing methods, motivating a unified taxonomy for model adaptation.

</details>


### [138] [Interpretable Stylistic Variation in Human and LLM Writing Across Genres, Models, and Decoding Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14111)
*Swati Rallapalli,Shannon Gallagher,Ronald Yurko,Tyler Brooks,Chuck Loughin,Michele Sezgin,Violet Turri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过大规模分析11种大语言模型在8种体裁和4种解码策略下的文本风格，发现模型类型和体裁对生成文本的风格影响大于提示和解码策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解人类写作与机器生成文本之间的文体差异，以应对大语言模型可能带来的滥用问题。

Method: 使用Douglas Biber的词汇语法和功能特征集，对人类文本和11种大语言模型生成的文本进行跨体裁、跨解码策略的大规模文体变异分析。

Result: 发现LLM生成文本的语言差异在不同生成条件下具有鲁棒性；体裁对文体特征的影响强于文本来源；聊天版本模型在文体空间中趋于聚类；模型本身比解码策略对风格影响更大。

Conclusion: 模型类型和体裁是塑造机器生成文本风格的关键因素，提示和解码策略的作用相对较小。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are now capable of generating highly fluent, human-like text. They enable many applications, but also raise concerns such as large scale spam, phishing, or academic misuse. While much work has focused on detecting LLM-generated text, only limited work has gone into understanding the stylistic differences between human-written and machine-generated text. In this work, we perform a large scale analysis of stylistic variation across human-written text and outputs from 11 LLMs spanning 8 different genres and 4 decoding strategies using Douglas Biber's set of lexicogrammatical and functional features. Our findings reveal insights that can guide intentional LLM usage. First, key linguistic differentiators of LLM-generated text seem robust to generation conditions (e.g., prompt settings to nudge them to generate human-like text, or availability of human-written text to continue the style); second, genre exerts a stronger influence on stylistic features than the source itself; third, chat variants of the models generally appear to be clustered together in stylistic space, and finally, model has a larger effect on the style than decoding strategy, with some exceptions. These results highlight the relative importance of model and genre over prompting and decoding strategies in shaping the stylistic behavior of machine-generated text.

</details>


### [139] [Correct Prediction, Wrong Steps? Consensus Reasoning Knowledge Graph for Robust Chain-of-Thought Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14121)
*Zipeng Ling,Shuliang Liu,Shenghong Fu,Yuehao Tang,Seonil Son,Yao Wan,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CRAFT框架，通过构建推理知识图谱并生成高质量推理路径，有效缓解LLM推理中的多种错误，显著提升逻辑与数学推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的推理过程存在复杂的错误，包括步骤内部错误和步骤间错误，且样本间差异大，现有方法难以有效纠正。

Method: 提出CRAFT框架，基于多个候选推理路径的共识部分构建推理知识图谱（RKG），并通过拓扑生成合成高质量推理路径。

Result: 在逻辑和数学推理基准上，平均标签预测准确率提升10%以上，推理路径质量在多个维度上均优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: CRAFT能有效缓解LLM推理中的多种复杂错误，显著提升推理能力和路径质量，且无需依赖标注指导即可实现性能突破。

Abstract: LLM reasoning traces suffer from complex flaws -- *Step Internal Flaws* (logical errors, hallucinations, etc.) and *Step-wise Flaws* (overthinking, underthinking), which vary by sample. A natural approach would be to provide ground-truth labels to guide LLMs' reasoning. Contrary to intuition, we show that this yields no improvement in reasoning ability. We then propose CRAFT, a unified framework that mitigates both types of Step flaws, which builds a Reasoning Knowledge Graph (RKG) based on the consensus parts of multiple candidate traces, and synthesizes a high-quality trace through topological generation. Our approach improves label-prediction accuracy by 10+% on average, and consistently outperforms all baselines across both logical and mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Further, detailed benchmark evaluation proves that our method also improves the quality of LLMs' reasoning traces in multiple dimensions.

</details>


### [140] [Rhetorical Questions in LLM Representations: A Linear Probing Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14128)
*Louie Hong Yao,Vishesh Anand,Yuan Zhuang,Tianyu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过线性探针分析大语言模型中修辞问句的表示，发现其信号在早期出现且主要由末尾词元稳定捕获，虽可跨数据集识别（AUROC 0.7-0.8），但不同数据集训练的探针揭示了不同的修辞现象，表明修辞问句由多个强调不同线索的线性方向编码，而非单一共享方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型如何在内部表示修辞问句，因其不为获取信息而是用于说服或表达立场，目前对此类结构的表征机制尚不清楚。

Method: 使用线性探针方法，在两个不同话语背景的社交媒体数据集上分析大语言模型中修辞问句的表示，并评估其跨数据集的可迁移性及探针行为的一致性。

Result: 修辞信号在模型早期层中即已显现，主要由最后词元稳定表征；在数据集内可与信息性问题线性区分，跨数据集检测AUROC达0.7-0.8，但不同数据集训练的探针在相同语料上的排序差异大（重叠率低于0.2），揭示出不同的修辞特征。

Conclusion: 大语言模型中修辞问句的表示并非单一共享方向，而是由多个强调不同线索（如话语层面立场或局部句法结构）的线性方向共同编码。

Abstract: Rhetorical questions are asked not to seek information but to persuade or signal stance. How large language models internally represent them remains unclear. We analyze rhetorical questions in LLM representations using linear probes on two social-media datasets with different discourse contexts, and find that rhetorical signals emerge early and are most stably captured by last-token representations. Rhetorical questions are linearly separable from information-seeking questions within datasets, and remain detectable under cross-dataset transfer, reaching AUROC around 0.7-0.8. However, we demonstrate that transferability does not simply imply a shared representation. Probes trained on different datasets produce different rankings when applied to the same target corpus, with overlap among the top-ranked instances often below 0.2. Qualitative analysis shows that these divergences correspond to distinct rhetorical phenomena: some probes capture discourse-level rhetorical stance embedded in extended argumentation, while others emphasize localized, syntax-driven interrogative acts. Together, these findings suggest that rhetorical questions in LLM representations are encoded by multiple linear directions emphasizing different cues, rather than a single shared direction.

</details>


### [141] [From Feelings to Metrics: Understanding and Formalizing How Users Vibe-Test LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14137)
*Itay Itzhak,Eliya Habba,Gabriel Stanovsky,Yonatan Belinkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了用户如何通过“vibe-testing”（非正式体验式评估）来评估大语言模型，并将其形式化为一种可系统分析的方法，提出了一种结合个性化提示和用户感知评价标准的评估流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现有基准测试难以反映模型在真实场景中的实用性，用户常依赖非正式的‘vibe-testing’进行评估，但这种方法缺乏结构和可复现性，因此需要对其进行系统化研究和形式化。

Method: 通过分析用户评估实践的调查数据以及来自博客和社交媒体的真实模型对比报告，将vibe-testing形式化为包含个性化测试内容与判断标准的两阶段过程，并设计了一个概念验证的评估流程。

Result: 实验表明，在编码任务中，使用个性化的提示和用户感知的主观评价标准会改变对模型的偏好，说明该方法能有效反映实际vibe-testing的效果。

Conclusion: 形式化的vibe-testing可以作为连接传统基准分数与真实使用体验的有效桥梁，为LLM评估提供了更具个性化和实用性的新路径。

Abstract: Evaluating LLMs is challenging, as benchmark scores often fail to capture models' real-world usefulness. Instead, users often rely on ``vibe-testing'': informal experience-based evaluation, such as comparing models on coding tasks related to their own workflow. While prevalent, vibe-testing is often too ad hoc and unstructured to analyze or reproduce at scale. In this work, we study how vibe-testing works in practice and then formalize it to support systematic analysis. We first analyze two empirical resources: (1) a survey of user evaluation practices, and (2) a collection of in-the-wild model comparison reports from blogs and social media. Based on these resources, we formalize vibe-testing as a two-part process: users personalize both what they test and how they judge responses. We then introduce a proof-of-concept evaluation pipeline that follows this formulation by generating personalized prompts and comparing model outputs using user-aware subjective criteria. In experiments on coding benchmarks, we find that combining personalized prompts and user-aware evaluation can change which model is preferred, reflecting the role of vibe-testing in practice. These findings suggest that formalized vibe-testing can serve as a useful approach for bridging benchmark scores and real-world experience.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Olfactory pursuit: catching a moving odor source in complex flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13121)
*Maurizio Carbone,Lorenzo Piro,Robin A. Heinonen,Luca Biferale,Massimo Cencini,Antonio Celani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在延迟和间歇性气味信号下如何有效追踪移动目标的问题，提出了一种结合信息增益与贪婪策略的混合策略，在不同运动持续性条件下均表现出近似最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在气味信号不稳定、延迟且混杂的环境中，传统方法难以高效定位移动目标，需要发展更鲁棒的搜索策略。

Method: 将嗅觉追踪建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，采用离散‘奔跑-翻滚’模型，通过求解贝尔曼方程获得准最优策略，并与Infotaxis等信息理论策略比较；提出一种结合信息增益与基于完全可观测问题的贪婪价值函数的混合策略。

Result: 纯探索性策略在目标频繁转向时表现良好，但在目标持续运动时失效；所提出的混合策略在各种持续时间下均接近最优，显著优于纯探索方法，并在更复杂的搜索场景中表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 预测性推断目标运动是实现高效嗅觉追踪的关键，该研究为信息贫乏、动态变化环境中的搜索提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Locating and intercepting a moving target from possibly delayed, intermittent sensory signals is a paradigmatic problem in decision-making under uncertainty, and a fundamental challenge for, e.g., animals seeking prey or mates and autonomous robotic systems. Odor signals are intermittent, strongly mixed by turbulent-like transport, and typically lag behind the true target position, thereby complicating localization. Here, we formulate olfactory pursuit as a partially observable Markov decision process in which an agent maintains a joint belief over the target's position and velocity. Using a discrete run-and-tumble model, we compute quasi-optimal policies by numerically solving the Bellman equation and benchmark them against well-established information-theoretic strategies such as Infotaxis. We show that purely exploratory policies are near-optimal when the target frequently reorients, but fail dramatically when the target exhibits persistent motion. We thus introduce a computationally efficient hybrid policy that combines the information-gain drive of Infotaxis with a "greedy" value function derived from an associated fully observable control problem. Our heuristic achieves near-optimal performance across all persistence times and substantially outperforms purely exploratory approaches. Moreover, our proposal demonstrates strong robustness even in more complex search scenarios, including continuous run-and-tumble prey motion with moderate persistence time, model mismatch, and more accurate plume dynamics representation. Our results identify predictive inference of target motion as the key ingredient for effective olfactory pursuit and provide a general framework for search in information-poor, dynamically evolving environments.

</details>


### [143] [Multi-modal panoramic 3D outdoor datasets for place categorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13142)
*Hojung Jung,Yuki Oto,Oscar M. Mozos,Yumi Iwashita,Ryo Kurazume*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了两个多模态全景3D户外数据集，用于语义地点分类，并在福冈市采集，分别包含650个密集点云和34,200个稀疏点云扫描，分类准确率分别达到96.42%和89.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了推动语义地点分类的研究，需要高质量、多样化的多模态3D数据集，特别是在不同密度点云条件下的实际场景数据。

Method: 构建了两个多模态全景3D户外数据集，一个使用FARO激光扫描仪获取高密度彩色和反射率点云及同步图像，另一个使用Velodyne激光扫描仪在行驶中获取稀疏反射率点云；并比较了多种语义地点分类方法。

Result: 实现了语义地点分类的最高准确率分别为96.42%（密集数据）和89.67%（稀疏数据）。

Conclusion: 所提出的MPO数据集为语义地点分类提供了有价值的资源，实验表明现有方法在密集和稀疏点云上均能取得良好性能，有助于自动驾驶与环境理解研究。

Abstract: We present two multi-modal panoramic 3D outdoor (MPO) datasets for semantic place categorization with six categories: forest, coast, residential area, urban area and indoor/outdoor parking lot. The first dataset consists of 650 static panoramic scans of dense (9,000,000 points) 3D color and reflectance point clouds obtained using a FARO laser scanner with synchronized color images. The second dataset consists of 34,200 real-time panoramic scans of sparse (70,000 points) 3D reflectance point clouds obtained using a Velodyne laser scanner while driving a car. The datasets were obtained in the city of Fukuoka, Japan and are publicly available in [1], [2]. In addition, we compare several approaches for semantic place categorization with best results of 96.42% (dense) and 89.67% (sparse).

</details>


### [144] [Weakly-supervised Learning for Physics-informed Neural Motion Planning via Sparse Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13204)
*Ruiqi Ni,Yuchen Liu,Ahmed H. Qureshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种层次化神经时间场（H-NTFields）方法，结合稀疏路线图的弱监督与物理信息PDE正则化，提升复杂环境中运动规划的鲁棒性和全局一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于物理信息的方法在复杂多房间环境中难以扩展，容易陷入局部极小且无法保证全局一致性。

Method: 提出H-NTFields框架，融合来自稀疏路线图的弱监督（提供通行时间上下界）和PDE损失（保证局部几何准确性和避障传播）。

Result: 在18个Gibson环境和真实机器人平台上实验表明，H-NTFields显著优于先前的物理信息方法，具备更强的鲁棒性和快速的摊销推理能力。

Conclusion: H-NTFields通过结合弱监督与物理约束，有效解决了高维复杂场景中运动规划的可扩展性与全局一致性问题。

Abstract: The motion planning problem requires finding a collision-free path between start and goal configurations in high-dimensional, cluttered spaces. Recent learning-based methods offer promising solutions, with self-supervised physics-informed approaches such as Neural Time Fields (NTFields) solving the Eikonal equation to learn value functions without expert demonstrations. However, existing physics-informed methods struggle to scale in complex, multi-room environments, where simply increasing the number of samples cannot resolve local minima or guarantee global consistency. We propose Hierarchical Neural Time Fields (H-NTFields), a weakly-supervised framework that combines weak supervision from sparse roadmaps with physics-informed PDE regularization. The roadmap provides global topological anchors through upper and lower bounds on travel times, while PDE losses enforce local geometric fidelity and obstacle-aware propagation. Experiments on 18 Gibson environments and real robotic platforms show that H-NTFields substantially improves robustness over prior physics-informed methods, while enabling fast amortized inference through a continuous value representation.

</details>


### [145] [Capability-Aware Heterogeneous Control Barrier Functions for Decentralized Multi-Robot Safe Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13245)
*Joonkyung Kim,Yanze Zhang,Wenhao Luo,Yiwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能力感知的异构控制屏障函数（CA-HCBF）框架，用于在异构多机器人系统中实现去中心化的安全控制与协调，兼顾安全性与任务效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化方法常假设机器人同质性，导致在异构系统中对安全要求的理解不一致，可能引发安全隐患或死锁问题。

Method: 通过规范变换和反步法构建统一的二阶仿射控制模型，并引入基于支持函数的方向性能力度量，按各机器人的运动能力分配避障责任，结合可行性感知裁剪机制确保约束可执行。

Result: 在最多30个异构机器人的仿真和实物实验中，该方法相比基线方法展现出更高的安全性和任务效率。

Conclusion: CA-HCBF能够有效实现异构多机器人系统的去中心化安全控制，且具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Safe navigation for multi-robot systems requires enforcing safety without sacrificing task efficiency under decentralized decision-making. Existing decentralized methods often assume robot homogeneity, making shared safety requirements non-uniformly interpreted across heterogeneous agents with structurally different dynamics, which could lead to avoidance obligations not physically realizable for some robots and thus cause safety violations or deadlock. In this paper, we propose Capability-Aware Heterogeneous Control Barrier Function (CA-HCBF), a decentralized framework for consistent safety enforcement and capability-aware coordination in heterogeneous robot teams. We derive a canonical second-order control-affine representation that unifies holonomic and nonholonomic robots under acceleration-level control via canonical transformation and backstepping, preserving forward invariance of the safe set while avoiding relative-degree mismatch across heterogeneous dynamics. We further introduce a support-function-based directional capability metric that quantifies each robot's ability to follow its motion intent, deriving a pairwise responsibility allocation that distributes the safety burden proportionally to each robot's motion capability. A feasibility-aware clipping mechanism further constrains the allocation to each agent's physically achievable range, mitigating infeasible constraint assignments common in dense decentralized CBF settings. Simulations with up to 30 heterogeneous robots and a physical multi-robot demonstration show improved safety and task efficiency over baselines, validating real-world applicability across robots with distinct kinematic constraints.

</details>


### [146] [GeoVision-Enabled Digital Twin for Hybrid Autonomous-Teleoperated Medical Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13248)
*Parham Kebria,Soheil Sabri,Laura J Brattain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GeoVision能力的数字孪生架构，用于混合自主-远程操作医疗响应系统，以提升灾害和基础设施有限环境中的应急医疗服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了改善在灾难影响区域和基础设施受限环境中紧急护理的支持效果，需要开发能够提供增强态势感知和决策支持的远程医疗响应系统。

Method: 该研究设计并实现了一个集成感知与自适应导航的数字孪生框架，并通过实时同步系统状态、环境动态、患者状况及任务目标来支持远程临床和操作用户。

Result: 所提出的框架能够为远程用户提供直观且持续更新的平台及其操作上下文的虚拟表示，从而提高情境意识和决策质量。

Conclusion: 数字孪生技术可以有效增强远程医疗响应系统的性能，在复杂环境下具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Remote medical response systems are increasingly being deployed to support emergency care in disaster-affected and infrastructure-limited environments. Enabled by GeoVision capabilities, this paper presents a Digital Twin architecture for hybrid autonomous-teleoperated medical response systems. The proposed framework integrates perception and adaptive navigation with a Digital Twin, synchronized in real-time, that mirrors system states, environmental dynamics, patient conditions, and mission objectives. Unlike traditional ground control interfaces, the Digital Twin provides remote clinical and operational users with an intuitive, continuously updated virtual representation of the platform and its operational context, enabling enhanced situational awareness and informed decision-making.

</details>


### [147] [Utilizing Inpainting for Keypoint Detection for Vision-Based Control of Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13309)
*Sreejani Chatterjee,Venkatesh Mullur,Abhinav Gandhi,Berk Calli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于自然视觉特征的新型视觉伺服框架，用于控制机器人机械臂的构型空间，无需依赖外部标记或精确标定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在无法或不希望使用外部标记的情况下，实现对机器人操作臂的鲁棒视觉控制。

Method: 通过在训练时利用ArUco标记并结合图像修复技术去除标记，生成自动标注的无标记机器人图像，训练关键点检测算法；引入运行时图像修复模型应对遮挡，并结合Unscented Kalman Filter提升关键点预测稳定性。

Result: 在全可见和部分遮挡条件下均实现了成功的机器人构型控制，验证了该方法在遮挡情况下的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了完全基于自然视觉特征、无需相机标定和精确机器人模型的视觉伺服控制，具有良好的实用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: In this paper we present a novel visual servoing framework to control a robotic manipulator in the configuration space by using purely natural visual features. Our goal is to develop methods that can robustly detect and track natural features or keypoints on robotic manipulators that would be used for vision-based control, especially for scenarios where placing external markers on the robot is not feasible or preferred at runtime. For the model training process of our data driven approach, we create a data collection pipeline where we attach ArUco markers along the robot's body, label their centers as keypoints, and then utilize an inpainting method to remove the markers and reconstruct the occluded regions. By doing so, we generate natural (markerless) robot images that are automatically labeled with the marker locations. These images are used to train a keypoint detection algorithm, which is used to control the robot configuration using natural features of the robot. Unlike the prior methods that rely on accurate camera calibration and robot models for labeling training images, our approach eliminates these dependencies through inpainting. To achieve robust keypoint detection even in the presence of occlusion, we introduce a second inpainting model, this time to utilize during runtime, that reconstructs occluded regions of the robot in real time, enabling continuous keypoint detection. To further enhance the consistency and robustness of keypoint predictions, we integrate an Unscented Kalman Filter (UKF) that refines the keypoint estimates over time, adding to stable and reliable control performance. We obtained successful control results with this model-free and purely vision-based control strategy, utilizing natural robot features in the runtime, both under full visibility and partial occlusion.

</details>


### [148] [Vectorizing Projection in Manifold-Constrained Motion Planning for Real-Time Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13323)
*Shrutheesh R Iyer,I-Chia Chang,Andrew Z. Liu,Yan Gu,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于CPU SIMD加速的流形约束运动规划器，通过并行化投影-based约束满足方法，实现了比现有技术快100-1000倍的速度，首次实现实时约束运动规划，并在真实人形机器人上验证了全身体 quasi-static 计划的实时生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流形约束运动规划方法在复杂系统（如人形机器人）上求解速度慢，通常需要数十秒，难以满足动态环境中的实时应用需求。

Method: 受硬件加速运动规划进展的启发，重新审视基于投影的约束满足方法，将其关键组件转化为可并行结构，并利用CPU的SIMD并行性实现高效计算。

Result: 相比当前最先进的方法，实现了100到1000倍的加速，能够在真实人形机器人上实现实时的全身准静态路径规划。

Conclusion: 该方法首次使实时约束运动规划成为可能，为复杂机器人系统在动态环境中的实际应用开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Many robot planning tasks require satisfaction of one or more constraints throughout the entire trajectory. For geometric constraints, manifold-constrained motion planning algorithms are capable of planning collision-free path between start and goal configurations on the constraint submanifolds specified by task. Current state-of-the-art methods can take tens of seconds to solve these tasks for complex systems such as humanoid robots, making real-world use impractical, especially in dynamic settings. Inspired by recent advances in hardware accelerated motion planning, we present a CPU SIMD-accelerated manifold-constrained motion planner that revisits projection-based constraint satisfaction through the lens of parallelization. By transforming relevant components into parallelizable structures, we use SIMD parallelism to plan constraint satisfying solutions. Our approach achieves up to 100-1000x speed-ups over the state-of-the-art, making real-time constrained motion planning feasible for the first time. We demonstrate our planner on a real humanoid robot and show real-time whole-body quasi-static plan generation. Our work is available at https://commalab.org/papers/mcvamp/.

</details>


### [149] [Boundary Sampling to Learn Predictive Safety Filters via Pontryagin's Maximum Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13325)
*James Dallas,Thomas Lew,John Talbot,Jonathan DeCastro,Somil Bansal,John Subosits*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于庞特里亚金最大值原理（PMP）引导数据采样的学习型安全过滤方法，用于提升高维系统中安全关键状态下的学习效率，并在自动驾驶赛车实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂高维系统中，传统学习方法难以高效采集接近安全边界的状态数据，导致安全过滤性能受限。因此需要一种能主动引导采样、聚焦安全临界区域的方法以提高学习效率和安全性。

Method: 利用庞特里亚金最大值原理（PMP）刻画勉强避免安全违规的边界轨迹，并以此指导数据采集；采用学习型Hamilton-Jacobi可达性分析，在安全临界区域集中学习资源，构建控制屏障值函数（CBVF），并直接用于安全过滤。

Result: 仿真与共享控制的自动驾驶赛车实验证明，PMP引导的采样显著提升了学习效率，实现了更快收敛、更低失败率和更优的安全集重建效果，单次运行时间约为3毫秒。

Conclusion: 通过结合最优控制理论与学习方法，该工作有效解决了高维系统中安全关键状态数据采样难的问题，为实时安全过滤提供了高效可靠的学习框架。

Abstract: Safety filters provide a practical approach for enforcing safety constraints in autonomous systems. While learning-based tools scale to high-dimensional systems, their performance depends on informative data that includes states likely to lead to constraint violation, which can be difficult to efficiently sample in complex, high-dimensional systems. In this work, we characterize trajectories that barely avoid safety violations using the Pontryagin Maximum Principle. These boundary trajectories are used to guide data collection for learned Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability, concentrating learning efforts near safety-critical states to improve efficiency. The learned Control Barrier Value Function is then used directly for safety filtering. Simulations and experimental validation on a shared-control automotive racing application demonstrate PMP sampling improves learning efficiency, yielding faster convergence, reduced failure rates, and improved safe set reconstruction, with wall times around 3ms.

</details>


### [150] [Singularity Avoidance in Inverse Kinematics: A Unified Treatment of Classical and Learning-based Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13405)
*Vishnu Rudrasamudram,Hariharasudan Malaichamee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述并统一了经典与基于学习的逆运动学方法，提出分类体系和基准测试协议，实验表明纯学习方法在奇异性问题上表现差，而结合经典优化的混合架构效果更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对经典奇异性鲁棒逆运动学与新兴学习方法的系统整合与比较，导致评估空白。

Method: 提出统一框架，涵盖雅可比正则化、黎曼操作性跟踪、约束优化及数据驱动方法，并建立基于几何结构保持和鲁棒性保证的分类体系；设计基准测试协议，在Franka Panda机械臂上评估12种IK求解器。

Result: 实验显示纯学习方法（如MLP）在良态目标下失败（成功率0%），而混合 warm-start 架构（如IKFlow、CycleIK、GGIK）通过经典 refinement 显著提升性能；DLS能从高达207mm初始误差收敛。

Conclusion: 纯学习逆运动学方法在奇异性处理上不可靠，需结合传统方法的混合架构以确保鲁棒性，未来需深入评估奇异性区域的表现。

Abstract: Singular configurations cause loss of task-space mobility, unbounded joint velocities, and solver divergence in inverse kinematics (IK) for serial manipulators. No existing survey bridges classical singularity-robust IK with rapidly growing learning-based approaches. We provide a unified treatment spanning Jacobian regularization, Riemannian manipulability tracking, constrained optimization, and modern data-driven paradigms. A systematic taxonomy classifies methods by retained geometric structure and robustness guarantees (formal vs. empirical). We address a critical evaluation gap by proposing a benchmarking protocol and presenting experimental results: 12 IK solvers are evaluated on the Franka Panda under position-only IK across four complementary panels measuring error degradation by condition number, velocity amplification, out-of-distribution robustness, and computational cost. Results show that pure learning methods fail even on well-conditioned targets (MLP: 0% success, approx. 10 mm mean error), while hybrid warm-start architectures - IKFlow (59% to 100%), CycleIK(0% to 98.6%), GGIK (0% to 100%) - rescue learned solvers via classical refinement, with DLS converging from initial errors up to 207 mm. Deeper singularity-regime evaluation is identified as immediate future work.

</details>


### [151] [Robust Energy-Aware Routing for Air-Ground Cooperative Multi-UAV Delivery in Wind-Uncertain Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13441)
*Tianshun Li,Hongliang Lu,Yanggang Sheng,Zhongzhen Wang,Haoang Li,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Battery-Efficient Routing (BER)的在线风险敏感规划框架，用于风敏感的卡车辅助无人机配送，通过动态能量图和实时风况感知显著提高了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径规划方法通常假设静态或确定性能耗模型，在动态风场环境中不可靠，难以确保无人机在不确定风况下的能量可行性和安全返航。

Method: 将问题建模为时变能量图上的路径规划，边权随风致气动效应动态变化；BER框架持续评估返航可行性，并平衡即时能耗与不确定性感知风险；结合任务分配、路径规划和去中心化轨迹执行的分层空地协同架构。

Result: 在Unreal Engine生成的合成环境和准真实风场日志上的仿真表明，BER相比静态和贪婪基线方法显著提升了任务成功率，减少了风致故障。

Conclusion: 在动态风场条件下，集成实时能量预算与环境感知对无人机配送规划至关重要，BER框架有效提升了系统的安全性与可靠性。

Abstract: Ensuring energy feasibility under wind uncertainty is critical for the safety and reliability of UAV delivery missions. In realistic truck-drone logistics systems, UAVs must deliver parcels and safely return under time-varying wind conditions that are only partially observable during flight. However, most existing routing approaches assume static or deterministic energy models, making them unreliable in dynamic wind environments. We propose Battery-Efficient Routing (BER), an online risk-sensitive planning framework for wind-sensitive truck-assisted UAV delivery. The problem is formulated as routing on a time dependent energy graph whose edge costs evolve according to wind-induced aerodynamic effects. BER continuously evaluates return feasibility while balancing instantaneous energy expenditure and uncertainty-aware risk. The approach is embedded in a hierarchical aerial-ground delivery architecture that combines task allocation, routing, and decentralized trajectory execution. Extensive simulations on synthetic ER graphs generated in Unreal Engine environments and quasi-real wind logs demonstrate that BER significantly improves mission success rates and reduces wind-induced failures compared with static and greedy baselines. These results highlight the importance of integrating real-time energy budgeting and environmental awareness for UAV delivery planning under dynamic wind conditions.

</details>


### [152] [RobotPan: A 360$^\circ$ Surround-View Robotic Vision System for Embodied Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13476)
*Jiahao Ma,Qiang Zhang,Peiran Liu,Zeran Su,Pihai Sun,Gang Han,Wen Zhao,Wei Cui,Zhang Zhang,Zhiyuan Xu,Renjing Xu,Jian Tang,Miaomiao Liu,Yijie Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合六台相机与LiDAR的全景视觉系统，并设计了RobotPan框架，用于实现实时、度量一致且紧凑的3D高斯表示，支持机器人在导航、操作和运动中的全景感知与渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人视觉接口通常视野狭窄或需手动切换多摄像头视角，导致操作中断和晕动症，难以满足实时全身部署的几何与实时性要求。

Method: 提出RobotPan，一种前馈框架，通过校准的稀疏视角输入预测度量缩放且紧凑的3D高斯表示；将多视角特征提升至统一的球面坐标表示，并利用分层球体素先验解码高斯，近处精细、远处粗糙以减少计算冗余；采用在线融合策略动态更新内容并抑制静态区域的无界增长。

Result: RobotPan在重建与新视角合成质量上优于现有前馈方法，同时生成的高斯数量显著更少，实现实时性能；并发布了一个面向360度新视角合成与度量3D重建的多传感器数据集。

Conclusion: RobotPan实现了高效、高质量的全景感知与3D表示，适用于实际机器人系统的实时部署，在导航、操控和运动任务中具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: Surround-view perception is increasingly important for robotic navigation and loco-manipulation, especially in human-in-the-loop settings such as teleoperation, data collection, and emergency takeover. However, current robotic visual interfaces are often limited to narrow forward-facing views, or, when multiple on-board cameras are available, require cumbersome manual switching that interrupts the operator's workflow. Both configurations suffer from motion-induced jitter that causes simulator sickness in head-mounted displays. We introduce a surround-view robotic vision system that combines six cameras with LiDAR to provide full 360$^\circ$ visual coverage, while meeting the geometric and real-time constraints of embodied deployment. We further present \textsc{RobotPan}, a feed-forward framework that predicts \emph{metric-scaled} and \emph{compact} 3D Gaussians from calibrated sparse-view inputs for real-time rendering, reconstruction, and streaming. \textsc{RobotPan} lifts multi-view features into a unified spherical coordinate representation and decodes Gaussians using hierarchical spherical voxel priors, allocating fine resolution near the robot and coarser resolution at larger radii to reduce computational redundancy without sacrificing fidelity. To support long sequences, our online fusion updates dynamic content while preventing unbounded growth in static regions by selectively updating appearance. Finally, we release a multi-sensor dataset tailored to 360$^\circ$ novel view synthesis and metric 3D reconstruction for robotics, covering navigation, manipulation, and locomotion on real platforms. Experiments show that \textsc{RobotPan} achieves competitive quality against prior feed-forward reconstruction and view-synthesis methods while producing substantially fewer Gaussians, enabling practical real-time embodied deployment. Project website: https://robotpan.github.io/

</details>


### [153] [RadarSplat-RIO: Indoor Radar-Inertial Odometry with Gaussian Splatting-Based Radar Bundle Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13492)
*Pou-Chun Kung,Yuan Tian,Zhengqin Li,Yue Liu,Eric Whitmire,Wolf Kienzle,Hrvoje Benko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高斯点阵（Gaussian Splatting, GS）的雷达束调整（radar BA）框架，首次实现了雷达数据的多帧联合优化，显著降低了雷达SLAM中的位姿漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达SLAM主要依赖帧间里程计，导致累积漂移严重；尽管闭环可修正长期误差，但受限于重访地点和地点识别能力，缺乏类似视觉BA的局部联合优化机制。

Method: 利用高斯点阵（GS）这一密集且可微分的场景表示方法，构建首个适用于雷达的束调整框架，联合优化雷达传感器位姿与场景几何结构，并使用完整的距离-方位-多普勒雷达数据进行局部窗口内的多帧优化。

Result: 在多个室内场景中，所提雷达BA方法相较之前的雷达-惯性里程计前端，平均绝对平移误差和旋转误差分别降低了90%和80%，显著提升了定位精度与系统鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将束调整引入雷达SLAM，验证了基于高斯点阵的雷达BA的有效性，为雷达主导的高精度定位提供了新思路。

Abstract: Radar is more resilient to adverse weather and lighting conditions than visual and Lidar simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM). However, most radar SLAM pipelines still rely heavily on frame-to-frame odometry, which leads to substantial drift. While loop closure can correct long-term errors, it requires revisiting places and relies on robust place recognition. In contrast, visual odometry methods typically leverage bundle adjustment (BA) to jointly optimize poses and map within a local window. However, an equivalent BA formulation for radar has remained largely unexplored. We present the first radar BA framework enabled by Gaussian Splatting (GS), a dense and differentiable scene representation. Our method jointly optimizes radar sensor poses and scene geometry using full range-azimuth-Doppler data, bringing the benefits of multi-frame BA to radar for the first time. When integrated with an existing radar-inertial odometry frontend, our approach significantly reduces pose drift and improves robustness. Across multiple indoor scenes, our radar BA achieves substantial gains over the prior radar-inertial odometry, reducing average absolute translational and rotational errors by 90% and 80%, respectively.

</details>


### [154] [Stability Principle Underlying Passive Dynamic Walking of Rimless Wheel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13530)
*Fumihiko Asano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了无轮缘轮在支撑阶段的稳定性，通过运动方程的线性化和能量守恒定律的关系分析，深化了对被动动态行走内在稳定性的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知无轮缘轮的被动步态具有渐近稳定性，但对其内在稳定性原理仍缺乏深入理解，因此需要进一步研究支撑阶段的稳定性机制。

Method: 基于运动方程的线性化方法，结合能量守恒定律进行数学分析，探讨稳定性与能量守恒之间的关系。

Result: 揭示了无轮缘轮在支撑阶段的稳定性与其能量恢复特性之间的内在联系，阐明了渐近稳定的本质原因。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析深化了对无轮缘轮被动行走稳定性的理解，为被动动态行走系统的设计提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Rimless wheels are known as the simplest model for passive dynamic walking. It is known that the passive gait generated only by gravity effect always becomes asymptotically stable and 1-period because a rimless wheel automatically achieves the two necessary conditions for guaranteeing the asymptotic stability; one is the constraint on impact posture and the other is the constraint on restored mechanical energy. The asymptotic stability is then easily shown by the recurrence formula of kinetic energy. There is room, however, for further research into the inherent stability principle. In this paper, we reconsider the stability of the stance phase based on the linearization of the equation of motion, and investigate the relation between the stability and energy conservation law. Through the mathematical analysis, we provide a greater understanding of the inherent stability principle.

</details>


### [155] [Evolvable Embodied Agent for Robotic Manipulation via Long Short-Term Reflection and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13533)
*Jianzong Wang,Botao Zhao,Yayun He,Junqing Peng,Xulong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大视觉-语言模型的可演化具身智能体（EEAgent）框架，通过长短期反思优化机制（LSTRO）实现机器人在复杂任务中的持续自我进化与性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人学习方法存在训练成本高、跨任务泛化能力差和缺乏可解释性等问题，难以实现通用机器人；现有基于提示的学习方法虽减少训练需求，但难以从成功与失败中提取有意义的经验用于持续进化。

Method: 提出EEAgent框架，利用大视觉-语言模型（VLMs）增强环境理解和策略规划，并设计长短期反思优化（LSTRO）机制，动态根据过往经验与新学到的知识优化提示，实现持续自我演化。

Result: 在六个VIMA-Bench任务上的评估显示，该方法达到新的最先进水平，在复杂场景中显著优于基线方法，提升了整体任务成功率。

Conclusion: EEAgent框架结合VLM与LSTRO机制，有效支持机器人基于经验反思实现无需大量训练的持续自我进化，推动了通用机器人向更高效、自适应方向发展。
